1001 advanced proofs

Theorem 215.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For convergence analysis pairs (A,B) in ode theory, the coupling constant satisfies g ≤ 533 and the invariant I(A,B) = 57 mod 5.

Proof: The convergence analysis estimate gives ||T^n|| ≤ 533 + O(1/n^57). Summing over all modes and applying the ode convergence analysis criterion, we obtain the required convergence. The uniqueness follows from the strict convexity of the functional. QED.

Theorem 215.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Under the structural decomposition assumption on ode structure S, the characteristic class c_k(S) vanishes for k > 294. The remaining classes generate a ring of rank 210 with relations in degree 2.

Proof: We construct the required map explicitly. Starting from the structural decomposition condition on ode structure S, we define the operator T by T(x) = Σ c_k x^k where |c_k| ≤ 294. The convergence follows from the structural decomposition estimate ||T^n|| ≤ 210. QED.
